    ABOUT CGN MINING                                                        關於中廣核礦業
    The Company is incorporated in the Cayman Islands and listed on         本公司註冊地為開曼群島，為一家在聯交
    the main board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 01164). The           所主板上市的公司（股票代號：01164）。
    principal activities of the Group are the development and trade of      本集團主要從事天然鈾資源的開發與貿
    natural uranium resources used by nuclear energy enterprises, with      易，旨在成為核電站天然鈾資源的主要供
    a view to being one of the top suppliers of natural uranium resources   貨商之一。
    for nuclear power plants.

                                                                        Chairman’s Statement
                                                                                     主席致辭

We understand that Shareholders’ support is the cornerstone of the       我們深知股東的支持是公司發展的基石，
Company’s development. In 2024, the Company achieved steady              2024年，公司實現業績穩步增長，在確保
growth in its results, and on the premise of ensuring the Company’s      公司可持續發展的前提下，董事會提議派
sustainable development, the Board proposed the payment of               發末期股息每股0.7港仙，以回饋廣大股
a final dividend of HK0.7 cents per Share in appreciation of the         東的信任與支持。2024年9月公司已派付
Shareholders’ trust and support. The Company has paid an interim         中期股息每股0.3港仙，於末期股息獲得
dividend of HK0.3 cents per Share in September 2024, and the             股東批准後，2024年全年平均分紅比例約
annual average dividend percentage for 2024 would be approximately       為22%。
22% upon the approval of the final dividend by the Shareholders.


For a long time, the Company has been focusing on its main business      長期以來，公司聚焦天然鈾資源開發與貿
of natural uranium resources development and trading, keeping            易的主業，著眼於世界範圍內的優質礦
its eye on high-quality mining resources worldwide, and exploring        山資源，發掘優質的投資機會，以形成合
quality investment opportunities to form a reasonable combination        理的在產、在建、高級和初級勘探項目梯
of in-production, under-construction, advanced and beginner              隊，滿足公司可持續發展需要。在全球核
level exploration projects to meet the needs of the Company’s            電發展態勢良好的背景下，全球鈾資源公
sustainable development. Against the background of favorable global      司加強鈾資源控制，加上更多鈾基金進入
development of nuclear energy, uranium resource companies around         天然鈾交易市場，鈾資源開發難度進一步
the world have tightened their control on uranium resources. Coupled     提升，這對我們資源獲取能力和投資策略
with the entry of more uranium funds into the natural uranium trading    提出了更高的要求。2025年，公司將在控
market, the difficulty in developing uranium resources has further       制好風險的前提下，採取併購、勘探開發
increased, placing a higher demand for our resource acquisition          並重策略，投資優質鈾礦項目，繼續對潛
capabilities and investment strategy. In 2025, under the premise of      在目標開展評估，期待盡快取得實質性進
risk control, the Company will adopt the strategy of focusing on both    展，做好資源開採續接，爭取更多投資收

while making investments in high-quality uranium projects, and
continue to make assessments on potential targets and look forward

     Anti-corruption and Whistle-blowing Policy                                 反貪污及舉報政策

     The Group does not tolerate any corruption, bribery, extortion, fraud      本集團於業務活動過程中並不容忍任何貪
     or money laundering during the course of its business activities.          污、賄賂、敲詐、詐騙或洗黑錢。因此，

     policy (the “Anti-Corruption and Whistle-blowing Policy”) which            及舉報政策」
                                                                                     ），透過參考相關法律及法規
     prohibits all forms of corruption practice having referenced the           禁止任何形式的貪污行為。反貪污及舉報
     relevant laws and regulations. The Anti-Corruption and Whistle-            政策構成本集團企業管治架構的重要一部
     blowing Policy forms an integral part of the Group’s corporate             分，當中載列本集團僱員須遵守的特定行
     governance framework, which sets out the specific behavioural              為指引以打擊貪污。反貪污及舉報政策旨
     guidelines that the employees of the Group must follow to combat           在為僱員及任何外部人士提供機密的舉報
     corruption. The Anti-Corruption and Whistle-blowing Policy is              渠道，以向本集團報告實際或存有懷疑的
     designed to provide the employees and any external parties with            非法活動及企業財務報告、內部控制或其

     or suspected illegal activities and misconducts in corporate financial     定期檢討及更新，以與適用法律及法規以

Risk management and internal control                                     風險管理與內部控制

Comprehensive risk management                                            全面風險管理

The Company has in essence established a relatively comprehensive        本公司已基本建立起較為完善的全面風險
risk management framework. With reference to the relevant risk           管理體系，以ISO31000:2018標準《風險
management requirements of the “Risk Management Guidelines”              管理指南》及企業管治守則有關風險管理
of ISO31000:2018 standard and the Corporate Governance Code,             的要求作為重要參考，對重大風險進行有
the Company effectively identified, evaluated and managed the            效辨認、評估及管理，嚴格落實《全面風
significant risks. It also strictly implemented the Comprehensive        險管理制度》，並將全面風險業務劃分為
Risk Management Policy. Meanwhile, the Company categorised               重大風險和一般風險，進行分類管理，同
its risks into significant risks and general risks for classification    時統籌管理項目投資風險等專項風險，建
and coordinated the management of specific risks such as project         立了相應的風險管理流程。董事會、管理
investment risk, and established corresponding risk management           層、風險管理部門及業務部門根據其於風
process. The Board, management, the risk management department           險管理中承擔的職責開展工作，董事會對
and business departments were responsible for their respective duties    風險管理及內部監控系統負責，有責任檢
within risk management. The Board was responsible for the risk           討該等制度的有效性，確保公司風險管理
management and the internal control system, with the duty to review      體系有效運作。
the effectiveness of such systems to ensure the efficient operation of
the Company’s risk management framework.


Such systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate the            該等制度旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務
risk of failing to achieve business objectives, and can only provide     目標的風險，而且只能就不會有重大的失
reasonable but not absolute assurance against material misstatement      實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。
or loss.


The Company handles and disseminates inside information in               本公司按列示於公司信息披露制度中的詳
accordance with the detailed procedures set out in the Company’s         細流程就內幕信息進行處理及發佈，以確
information disclosure policy, to ensure that inside information could   保內幕信息被及時識別並確保有關內幕信
be promptly identified and remain confidential until the disclosure      息於獲得適當批准前保持保密，有關信息

Cultivating talents                                                        人才培養

In order to promote the continuous growth of the Company and               為推動公司持續成長和維持市場競爭力，

to the core philosophy of “cultivating people, not just training           訓人」的核心理念，落實「培訓－考核－授
them” and implemented the fundamental requirements of “training,           權－上崗」的全員培訓、授權上崗、終身
assessment, authorization and employment” in the training of all           學習的基本要求，通過吸收借鑒國內國際
employees ensuring lifelong learning and authorized onboarding.            人才培養經驗和良好實踐，結合自身發展
By absorbing the experience and best practices of domestic and             特點，建立起有效的自主化人才培養體系
international talent development, the Company has and integrating          和規範的培訓管理制度。
them with its own development characteristics established an
effective independent talent development system and a standardized
training management system.


The Company formulates a annual detailed tiered training plan to           公司每年度制定詳細的分級培訓計劃，通
improve the quality and skills of managers and employees at all            過面授培訓、網絡培訓、面授及網絡混合
levels through face-to-face training, online training, and a blended       式培訓的方式，提升各層級管理者和員工
approach. This effectively meets Company’s talent needs for stable         的素質和技能，有效滿足了公司穩定發展
development.                                                               對人才的需求。


In the future, the Company will continue to invest in talent               未來公司將持續對人才培養方面進行投

sustainable development of the Company.


Remuneration system                                                        薪酬體系

To effectively attract high-quality talent and unlock employees’           為有效吸引高素質人才，激發員工潛能，
potential, the Company established a competitive remuneration              公司結合行業特點，搭建了富有競爭力
system tailored to the characteristics of the industry. The remuneration   的薪酬體系。薪酬體繫著重體現崗位價值
system emphasizes the value of positions and individual contributions      和個人貢獻，鼓勵員工長期為企業服務，
encouraging employees to serve the Company long-term and jointly           共同致力於企業的不斷成長和可持續性發
commit to its continuous growth and sustainable development while          展，同時共享企業發展所帶來的成果。薪
sharing in the fruits of the Company’s development. Remuneration is        酬主要由月薪、績效獎金、加班工資、津
primarily consists of monthly salary, performance bonus, over-time         貼與補貼和福利構成。董事薪酬亦參考本
pay, allowance and subsidy and benefits. Directors’ remuneration           公司薪酬政策及董事職責釐定。
also takes into consideration the remuneration policy of the Company
and the duties of the Directors.


In addition to the establishment of social security benefits for           在為員工建立社會保障的同時，公司還致
employees, the Company is also committed to building the                   力於建立公司內部的福利體系，以提高員
Company’s internal welfare system to improve the welfare of                工的福利待遇，公司亦關注員工健康，每
employees. The Company prioritizes the health of employees, and            年組織員工健康體檢。
organizes annual health checkups for employees.

The Company has adopted a dividend policy, pursuant to which                本公司已經採納股息政策，據此，可讓股
Shareholders are entitled to participate in the Company’s profits           東分享本公司溢利的同時保留充足儲備供
whilst retaining adequate reserves for future growth. The declaration       未來增長所用。本公司宣派及派付股息亦

      responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going      估    貴集團持續經營的能力，並在適用情
      concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern      況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以及
      and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors      使用持續經營為會計基礎，除非董事有意
      either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have no   將    貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他
      realistic alternative but to do so.                                       實際的替代方案。

STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the       我們的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否不

a body, in accordance with our agreed terms of engagement, and            款僅向   閣下（作為一個整體）報告，除此
for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or          之外，本報告不作其他用途。我們概不就
accept liability to any other person for the contents of this report.     本報告內容對任何其他人士負責或承擔責

always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements       錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,             起，如果合理預期其單獨或匯總起來可能

influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these     表所作出的經濟決定，則被視為重大錯誤
consolidated financial statements.                                        陳述。

throughout the audit. We also:                                            疑態度。我們亦：

      1.     GENERAL                                                         1.   一般資料
             CGN Mining Company Limited (the “Company”) was                       中廣核礦業有限公司（

4.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICY                                       4.   重大會計政策資料
     INFORMATION
     (a)   Basis of consolidation                                          (a)    綜合基準

           The consolidated financial statements comprise the                     綜合財務報表包括本集團
           financial statements of the Group. Inter-company                       的財務報表。集團內公司
           transactions and balances between group companies                      間所有交易、結存及未變
           together with unrealised profits are eliminated in full                現溢利已在編製綜合財務
           in preparing the consolidated financial statements.                    報表時悉數抵銷。除非交
           Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the                       易提供證據顯示已轉讓資
           transaction provides evidence of impairment on the                     產有所減值，否則未變現
           asset transferred, in which case the loss is recognised                虧損亦會撤銷，在此情況
           in profit or loss.                                                     下，虧損於損益確認。


     (b)   Subsidiaries                                                    (b)    附屬公司

           A subsidiary is an investee over which the Company                     附屬公司指本公司能對其
           is able to exercise control. The Company controls                      行使控制權之被投資方。
           an investee if all three of the following elements are                 倘達成以下三項條件，本
           present: (i) power over the investee, (ii) exposure, or                公司取得被投資方之控制
           rights, to variable returns from the investee, and (iii)               權：(i)有權控制投資對象，
           the ability to use its power to affect those variable                  (ii)對來自投資對象之浮動回
           returns. Control is reassessed whenever facts and                      報享有承擔或權利，以及
           circumstances indicate that there may be a change in                   (iii)能運用對投資對象之權
           any of these elements of control.                                      力以影響其所得回報。倘
                                                                                  有事實及情況顯示任何有
                                                                                  關控制條件改變，則重新
                                                                                  評估有關控制權。


           In the Company’s statement of financial position,                      於附屬公司之投資按成本
           investments in subsidiaries are stated at cost less                    減減值虧損（如有）列入本公
           impairment loss, if any. The results of subsidiaries                   司之財務狀況表。本公司
           are accounted for by the Company on the basis of                       按已收及應收股息基準對
           dividend received and receivable.                                      附屬公司之業績進行會計
                                                                                  處理。

                     recognised unless there is an obligation to make good                     於聯營公司之權益的虧損

           (iv)     Effective interest method                                           (iv)   實際利率法

                    The effective interest method is a method of                               實際利率法為計算
                    calculating the amortised cost of a financial                              金融資產或金融負
                    asset or financial liability and of allocating                             債的攤銷成本以及
                    interest income or interest expense over the                               於相關期間分配利
                    relevant period. The effective interest rate                               息收入或利息支出
                    is the rate that exactly discounts estimated                               的方法。實際利率
                    future cash receipts or payments through the                               乃為在金融資產或
                    expected life of the financial asset or liability, or                      負債的預計年期或
                    where appropriate, a shorter period.                                       （如 適 用）較 短 期 間
                                                                                               內能夠精確地將估
                                                                                               計未來現金收款或
                                                                                               付款貼現的利率。


           (v)      Equity instruments                                                  (v)    權益工具

                    Equity instruments issued by the Group are                                 本集團所發行權益
                    recorded at the proceeds received, net of direct                           工具以扣減直接發
                    issue costs.                                                               行成本後所收取的
                                                                                               所得款項記賬。


           (vi)     Derecognition                                                       (vi)   終止確認

                    The Group derecognises a financial asset when                              當與金融資產有關
                    the contractual rights to the future cash flows                            之未來現金流合約
                    in relation to the financial asset expire or when                          權利屆滿時，或當
                    the financial asset has been transferred and the                           金融資產已轉讓，
                    transfer meets the criteria for derecognition in                           且該轉讓按照香港
                    accordance with HKFRS 9.                                                   財務報告準則第9號
                                                                                               符合終止確認標準
                                                                                               時，本集團會終止
                                                                                               確認金融資產。


                    Financial liabilities are derecognised when the                            金融負債於相關合
                    obligation specified in the relevant contract is                           約內訂明之責任解
                    discharged, cancelled or expires.                                          除、註銷或屆滿時
                                                                                               終止確認。

             (i)     Inventories                                                      (i)   存貨

                     Inventories are initially recognised at cost, and                      存貨初步按成本確認，其
                     subsequently at the lower of cost and net realisable                   後按成本與可變現淨值兩
                     value. Cost comprises all costs of purchase, costs of                  者之較低者入賬。成本包
                     conversion and other costs incurred in bringing the                    括所有採購成本、轉換成
                     inventories to their present location and condition. Cost              本及使存貨達至目前地點
                     is calculated using the weighted average method. Net                   及狀態而產生的其他成
                     realisable value represents the estimated selling price                本。成本使用加權平均法
                     in the ordinary course of business less the estimated                  計算。可變現淨值指於一
                     costs of completion and the estimated costs necessary                  般業務過程中的估計售價
                     to make the sale.                                                      減去完成估計成本及進行
                                                                                            銷售所需的估計成本。

   The Group measures the loss allowance for trade receivables            本集團計量的應收賬款虧損撥備等
   at an amount equal to lifetime expected credit losses (“ECLs”).        於全期預期信貸虧損（
                                                                                   「預期信貸虧
   The ECLs on trade receivables are estimated using an                   損」
                                                                           ）。應收賬款之預期信貸虧損乃
   individual basis by reference to past default experience and           經參考債務人過往違約記錄及信
   creditworthiness of the debtor and an analysis of the debtor’s         譽以及對債務人現時財務狀況的分
   current financial position, adjusted for factors that are specific     析後採用個別基準估計，並就債務
   to the debtors, general economic conditions of the industry            人的特定因素、債務人營運所處行
   in which the debtors operate and an assessment of both the             業的整體經濟情況以及對報告日期
   current as well as the forecast direction of conditions at the         現時和預測狀況方向的評估予以調
   reporting date.                                                        整。


   For other receivables, the Group measures the loss allowance           就其他應收款項而言，本集團計量
   equal to 12-month ECLs, unless when there is a significant             與12個月預期信貸虧損等額的虧損
   increase in credit risk since initial recognition, the Group           撥備，除非信貸風險自首次確認以
   recognises lifetime ECLs. The assessment of whether lifetime           來顯著增加，則本集團會確認全期
   ECLs should be recognised is based on significant increase             預期信貸虧損。評估是否應確認全
   in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial         期預期信貸虧損，乃基於自首次確
   recognition.                                                           認起出現違約的可能性或風險是否
                                                                          顯著增加。


   There has been no change in the estimation techniques or               於本報告期內所作估算技術或重大
   significant assumptions made during the current reporting              假設並無任何變動。
   period.

      41. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               41. 財務風險管理
             The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments          在本集團日常業務過程中，本集團
             in the normal course of the Group’s business are credit risk,          來自金融工具的主要風險為信貸風
             liquidity risk, interest rate risk and currency risk.                  險、流動資金風險、利率風險及貨
                                                                                    幣風險。


             (a)     Credit risk                                                    (a)   信貸風險

                     The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk which                    因交易方未能履行責任而
                     will cause a financial loss to the Group due to failure to           令本集團蒙受財務虧損之
                     discharge an obligation by the counterparties is arising             最高信貸風險乃來自綜合
                     from the carrying amount of the respective recognised                財務狀況表所載列相關已
                     financial assets as stated in the consolidated statement             確認金融資產之賬面值。
                     of financial position.


                     In order to minimise the credit risk, the management of              為將信貸風險降至最低，
                     the Group has delegated a team responsible to ensure                 本集團管理層已委派一支
                     that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts.             團隊負責確保跟進收回逾
                     In addition, the Group reviews the recoverability of                 期債項之情況。此外，本
                     each individual trade debt at the end of each reporting              集團會於各報告期末檢討
                     period to ensure that adequate impairment losses are                 各個別應收賬款之可收回
                     made for irrecoverable amounts.                                      性，確保就不可收回金額
                                                                                          作出足夠減值虧損。


                     For trade receivables, the Group has applied the                     就應收賬款而言，本集團
                     simplified approach in HKFRS 9 to measure the loss                   已應用香港財務報告準則
                     allowance at lifetime ECLs. The Group determines                     第9號的簡化方法以按全
                     the ECLs for customer with significant balances                      期預期信貸虧損計量虧損
                     by using an individual basis, estimated based on                     撥備。本集團對具重大結
                     historical credit loss experience, as well as the general            存客戶按個別基礎估算，
                     economic conditions of the industry in which the                     以債務人過往信貸虧損經
                     debtors operate. In this regard, the directors of the                驗及債務人經營行業的整
                     Company consider that the Group has a low historical                 體經濟狀況釐定預期信貸
                     credit loss experience and there was no significant and              虧損。就此而言，本公司
                     adverse changes in the general economic conditions                   的董事認為本集團過往信
                     of the industry in what the debtors operate, thus the                貸虧損為低，由於債務人
                     management considered the credit risk is not material.               經營行業的整體經濟狀況
                                                                                          並無重大不利變動，因此
                                                                                          管理層認為信貸風險不重
                                                                                          大。

41. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)                               41. 財務風險管理（續）
    (a)   Credit risk (Continued)                                          (a)       （續）
                                                                                  信貸風險

          For other receivables, amounts due from fellow                          就其他應收款項而言，應
          subsidiaries included in bank balances and amount                       收同系附屬公司的款項包
          due from an intermediate holding company, the internal                  括銀行結存及應收一間中
          credit rating was ranked as “performing” as set out on                  間控股公司款項，內部信
          page 225. Based on the strong financial position and                    貸評級獲評為「良好」
                                                                                           （如第

42. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              42. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the            本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團
   Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                 旗下實體能夠持續經營業務，並透
   maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation          過更完善平衡債務及權益結餘，以
   of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy            為股東帶來最大回報。本集團整體
   remains unchanged from prior year.                                      策略與去年維持不變。


   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which          本集團之資本結構包括淨負債（包
   includes loans from a fellow subsidiary and loans from an               括應收一間同系附屬公司貸款及來
   intermediate holding company, bank borrowings, net of cash              自一間中間控股公司之貸款及銀行
   and cash equivalents and equity attributable to owners of               借款），扣除現金及現金等值項目
   the Company, comprising issued share capital and reserves.              以及本公司擁有人應佔權益（當中
   The directors of the Company review the capital structure on            包括已發行股本及儲備）。本公司
   a regular basis. As a part of this review, the directors of the         董事定期檢討資本結構。作為該檢
   Company consider the cost of capital and the risks associated           討其中一環，本公司董事考慮資本
   with each class of capital, and take appropriate actions to             成本及與各類資本相關的風險，並
   adjust the Group’s capital structure.                                   就此採取適當行動調整本集團資本
                                                                           結構。


   Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors              與業內其他公司做法一致，本集團
   capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated     以資本負債比率監察資本。該比率
   as net debt divided by total capital. Net debt is calculated as         以淨負債除以資本總額計算。淨負
   total borrowings less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is       債按借貸總額減現金及現金等值項
   calculated as equity as shown in the consolidated statement             目計算。資本總額按綜合財務狀況
   of financial position plus net debt.                                    表所列的權益加上淨負債計算。


   The gearing ratios at 31 December 2024 and 2023 were as                 於2024年及2023年12月31日的資
   follows:                                                                本負債比率如下：

      「tU」                                          指         噸金屬鈾。

       “U3O8”                                       means     Triuranium octaoxide, a compound of uranium present as an olive
                                                              green to black, odorless solid. It is one of the more popular forms
                                                              of yellowcake and is transported between mills and refineries in this
                                                              form.
      「U3O8」                                        指         八氧化三鈾，一種鈾的化合物，呈橄欖綠至黑色，無味的固體，為
                                                              比較受歡迎的黃餅形式之一，並以該形式在工廠及煉油廠之間運
                                                              輸。

      “UK”                                          means     the United Kingdom of Great Britain and Northern Ireland.
      「英國」                                          指         大不列顛及北愛爾蘭聯合王國。

      “US”                                          means     the United States of America.
      「美國」                                          指         美利堅合眾國。

      “USD” or “US$”                                means     United States dollars, the lawful currency of the US.
      「美元」或「US$」                                    指         美元，美國之法定貨幣。

       “UxC”                                        means     UxC, LLC, one of the leading providers of uranium prices and an
                                                              independent third party.
      「UxC」                                         指         UxC, LLC，為鈾價格的領先提供者之一，並為獨立第三方。

       “Zhalpak Deposit”                            means     the uranium deposit located in Sozak district, Kazakhstan, which is
                                                              owned and operated by Ortalyk.
      「扎礦」                                          指         位於哈薩克斯坦索扎克地區的鈾礦，由奧公司擁有及管理。
